Плюс критерий отказа от проекта. Triggers on: баланс, скучно, неинтересно, оптимальный ход очевиден, играть незачем, экономика игры, сложность, награждает ли мастерство, симуляция игры, телеметрия. Usar para gargalos de progressão, escolhas dominantes, grind e curvas de desafio; não confundir bot perfeito com jogador nem aplicar ajustes sem escopo autorizado. - **gameplay-behavior**: Проектирование и программирование трансляции пользовательского ввода, искусственного интеллекта, систем состояний персонажей, динамического создания типов сущностей и безопасных песочниц скриптовой логики. ([LeonidLR/Godot_Test_01](https://github.com/LeonidLR/Godot_Test_01/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/gameplay-behavior))
- **gameplay-blade-trails**: 当挥剑方向不清或拖尾断裂、串段和残留时，组织剑尖采样、弧形网格、尾端渐隐与纹理遮罩；轨迹只表达动作，不替代攻击判定。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-blade-trails))

- **gameplay-capture**: Take a picture or a movie of any section of OpenGGF gameplay (any game, zone, act, position, width, donor or team) driven by a BizHawk input log, with a per-frame state CSV that says which frame to look at. - **gameplay-ceiling-corner-correction**: 当上升角色轻擦顶角便被截断跳跃时，评估受限的横向角落修正及目标位置可达性；不处理输入早晚、缩小受伤区域或允许穿过实体。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-ceiling-corner-correction))

- **gameplay-contextual-ui**: 为游戏内按钮、按住操作、拾取/近物扫描提示、指令选择及紧凑设置面板区分可用、焦点、执行中、成功/失败，并组织菜单非物理退出；卡牌布局、对话、机关、连接和切场使用独立入口。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-contextual-ui))
- **gameplay-cooldown-ui**: 当技能倒计时、填充和实际可用时刻不一致时，校对冷却时钟与就绪状态；不把恢复动画结束作为技能规则，也不处理资源量或按住蓄力。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-cooldown-ui))
- **gameplay-creator-assistant**: Use this skill whenever the user wants to build, scaffold, prototype, or start a WHOLE GAME — or stand up the core gameplay/combat systems for one — in a project that uses the UGAS gameplay spec. Especialmente útil para aventuras de mineração, ação rítmica e roguelites; não implementa mudanças automaticamente. - **gameplay-crouch-zone-assistance**: 为已有蹲伏控制的玩家角色添加关卡碰撞区域辅助：进入低矮通道时贡献模拟蹲伏请求，复用原控制器并安全释放；不用于 NPC 决策、通用输入系统或边缘停挂。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-crouch-zone-assistance))
- **gameplay-cut-surface-rendering**: 当游戏切割产生开放截面、错误外皮材质或圆形木芯不贴合不规则轮廓时，组织新暴露面的封口与材质边界校对；不提供切割触发、通用三角化或物理断裂算法。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-cut-surface-rendering))
- **gameplay-dash-feedback**: 当冲刺速度存在但路径、起止或角色轮廓难读时，组合方向形变、残像与速度线并做样式对照；不因幽灵外观新增无敌或改变实际移动规则。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-dash-feedback))
- **gameplay-dash-roll-momentum**: 仅在明确要求真实移动规则时，实现向下冲刺接地转翻滚的速度交接，以及翻滚中冲刺消费储存动量的短时加速；不用于普通冲刺残像或落地重量润色。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-dash-roll-momentum))
- **gameplay-decorative-waterfall**: 为明确选择装饰性常流水的场景实现半砖湖缘触发、空中瀑布绘制与地面截断，保持源湖和下游液体量不受影响；不用于真实液体转移、水洼贴地网格或机器表面动画。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-decorative-waterfall))
- **gameplay-deformable-support**: 当车轮等载荷需要在可弯曲、可压缩的承载面上保持接触时，设计连接刚体层与层间弹簧的局部原型及稳定性验收；不是固定碰撞体的视觉压扁、通用软体算法或物性标定教程。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-deformable-support))
- **gameplay-design**: 玩法内容策划技能。生成核心循环、战斗系统、成长系统等玩法设计文档， 并提供对应的 GDScript 代码模板。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/plugins/codebuddy-plugins-official/plugins/godot-mcp/skills/godot-dev/level-3-orchestrators/game-planning/gameplay-design) · ★455 · 5 copies)

- **gameplay-design**: Create or revise game actions, controls, actor behavior and knowledge, spaces, encounters, shared timing, or free audiovisual play. Use for an executable action contract, behavior, layout, encounter or controllable phrase. Skip pure economy, lore, subscription policy, technical refactors and wording changes that leave these game relationships unchanged. ([Muratovnik/gamedesign-skills](https://github.com/Muratovnik/gamedesign-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-design))
- **gameplay-design-orchestrator**: 当游戏创意仍处于完整规格说明的上游阶段，需要顶层的游戏玩法设计流程时使用： 锁定方向、按序执行阶段，并整合为一个游戏玩法设计包。 这是全新游戏设计工作的常规入口。不适用于非游戏产品设计或直接实现。 ([W-ChouYing/AISkills](https://github.com/W-ChouYing/AISkills/tree/HEAD/02-game-design-pipeline/gameplay-design-orchestrator) · ★27)
- **gameplay-design-verification**: 用可计算的量回答玩法设计的两个硬问题——① 设计期：这个玩法到底有没有决策空间（写任何 UI 之前，用 随机/贪心/规划/全知 四层策略跑分布，测规划增益、技能表达、UX 体检三项指标；含十条可复用判据，以及难度「内容带≠阶梯」、通过线画在哪、数值可达性等设计错误）② 实现期：AI 真的按最优策略行动吗（先证局面结构性质、再写闭式解，用暴力博弈树 DP 逐个状态交叉验证，从交付文件抽取求解器代码块防测试漂移，双方最优自对弈端到端对账，UI 门控状态机单独测）。含两侧统一的断言纪律与变异测试（四种失效模式、等价变异必须存活、假断言三形态）。当用户说"这个玩法没深度""高手新手分数差不多""随便玩和认真玩一样""一局只有两三步""关数太少""太简单""几秒钟就玩完了""加了限时还是没深度""设计新的策略/资源管理/卡牌/规划/推理/解谜类玩法""优化 AI 让它走最优解""AI 不够聪明/像在乱走""这个博弈题的 AI 是错的"时使用。 ([aihaisi/aihaisi-workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/aihaisi/aihaisi-workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/gameplay-design-verification) · ★1)
- **gameplay-designer**: Gameplay Designer (Star-Lord): The fun engineer. Dispatch when the War Room seats this role. ([nx9161/war-room-os](https://github.com/nx9161/war-room-os/tree/HEAD/office/skills/gameplay-designer))
- **gameplay-dialogue-presentation**: 当逐字对话、标点节奏、情绪强调或重显后的文字进度不清时组织文本显示与动画生命周期；区分显隐、句子进度和真正继续操作，不接管剧情分支与 NPC 交互规则。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-dialogue-presentation))
- **gameplay-dynamic-carry-alignment**: 实现可变长短、粗细的世界物体携带：按重量分组选择姿态，校准局部长轴、厚度轴和锚点，按实际截面调整手部贴合并在放下时清理绑定；不用于固定工具挥动或物品入库反馈。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-dynamic-carry-alignment))

- **gameplay-engineer**: Phaser 3 TypeScript 게임의 룸·전투·소환·조합·분대 UI 기능을 최소 변경으로 구현한다. 실제 코드 수정 요청 시 사용한다. ([jjhnom/toyPjt](https://github.com/jjhnom/toyPjt/tree/HEAD/rnd/.agents/skills/gameplay-engineer))
- **gameplay-evolution-review**: Use when asked to simulate, evolve, playtest, balance, or evaluate a game/prototype's progression, economy, pacing, player personas, failure routes, exploits, or long-run gameplay experience. Não use para validar apenas contratos de mecânica, código sem execução ou opinião baseada só em documentação. ([Misael-art/genesis-forge](https://github.com/Misael-art/genesis-forge/tree/HEAD/tools/sgdk_wrapper/.agent/skills/design/gameplay-experience-reviewer))
- **gameplay-experiments**: Explore Sporefall Station's world in the ECS debugger, compose deterministic scenarios (salvages, set-pieces, emergent-mechanic tests), narrate them on screen with the annotation system, and produce annotated screenshot/video recordings. Use when asked to "try out" gameplay ideas, design multi-stage co-op scenarios, discover emergent mechanics, or make annotated gameplay reels. ([redaphid/sporefall-station](https://github.com/redaphid/sporefall-station/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gameplay-experiments) · ★2)
- **gameplay-facing-relative-light-aim**: 用移动决定角色面向，让鼠标只在面向附近的有限角域控制手电方向，并独立开关灯；适用于受限照明控制，不负责武器瞄准、分件姿态或光照阴影算法。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-facing-relative-light-aim))
- **gameplay-factory**: Create or continue gameplay work with continuous primary-agent authorship, tool-owned checkpoints and independent boundary reviews. Resolve project state first; preserve existing CLI and historical formats. ([csonic0926/game_studio_factory](https://github.com/csonic0926/game_studio_factory/tree/HEAD/gameplay/skills/gameplay-factory))
- **gameplay-fantasy-loop-designer**: 当 $gameplay-design-orchestrator 已完成规范化阶段、且本循环设计阶段 恰好是下一步时使用；或者用于修订已锁定的游戏玩法包的循环层。 不要将其作为自由入口的构思技能使用。 ([W-ChouYing/AISkills](https://github.com/W-ChouYing/AISkills/tree/HEAD/02-game-design-pipeline/gameplay-fantasy-loop-designer) · ★27)
- **gameplay-feature**: Implement complete SS14 features across ownership, ECS, networking, resources, localization, UI, and tests. ([ArcaneSS14/arcane-new](https://github.com/ArcaneSS14/arcane-new/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/gameplay-feature) · ★12)
- **gameplay-first**: A store-page trailer that shows real play from the first seconds: what you do, how it feels, why it is different. Use when the brief kind is gameplay, when it is for a Steam page, or when the request mentions gameplay, a store page, or "何をするゲームか". ([eiei114/shotpilot](https://github.com/eiei114/shotpilot/tree/HEAD/references/skills/gameplay-first))
- **gameplay-fixed-camera-art-projection**: 在固定构图相机下，将透视插画贴合简单盒体或圆柱，保留场景深度与遮挡；适用于绘画式环境资产，不用于自由环绕、多面投影或角色朝向相机的 billboard。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-fixed-camera-art-projection))
- **gameplay-floating-numbers**: 在瞬时伤害、治疗或得分数字缺少层次、遮挡对象或无法区分事件类型/暴击时设计浮字实例与密集事件布局；共享表现，不混同业务结算或结果总分计数。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-floating-numbers))
- **gameplay-foliage-rustle-response**: 为穿过或撞入植被的接触音原型接入既有移动速度、强度与方向，以有界的对象级响应替代每次相同的沙沙声；不负责植被形变或断裂倒伏阶段音频。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-foliage-rustle-response))
- **gameplay-framework**: Implement Unreal's gameplay framework in C++ — GameInstance, AGameModeBase/AGameMode, AGameStateBase/AGameState, APlayerController, APawn/ACharacter, APlayerState, and AHUD — including the server-only spawn/login flow, possession, controller-pawn lifecycle, and which class each piece of logic belongs in. Use when setting up game rules, default pawn/controller classes, player login/spawn/possession, replicated game or player state, match-state machines, respawn logic, or deciding "where does this code live?" ([Kright/unreal-engine-vite-skills](https://github.com/Kright/unreal-engine-vite-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/core/gameplay-framework) · ★1)
- **gameplay-framework**: The gameplay framework is the set of base classes Unreal spawns and wires together to run a game. Choosing the *right* class for each piece of logic is the single most important design decision in UE gameplay code. ([ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game](https://github.com/ziyarduc/Baran-bk-game/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/gameplay-framework) · ★1)
- **gameplay-gait-step-scheduling**: 当程序化步态在启动/转向时脚追赶身体，或多脚各自驱动髋部造成冲突时，协调预测迈步、选腿与共享髋运动；两分支按问题选用，不是方向换图、装饰跟随或物理平衡求解器。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-gait-step-scheduling))
- **gameplay-game-design**: Complete game design document for BlastSimulator2026: satirical open-pit mine management game. Covers core gameplay loop, mining mechanics, economy, events, corruption, world generation, material catalogs, weather, safety, campaign, save system, and win/lose conditions. Use when implementing or modifying any game mechanic or feature. ([Nico2398/BlastSimulator2026](https://github.com/Nico2398/BlastSimulator2026/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/gameplay-game-design) · 3 copies)
- **gameplay-gamepad-pitch-limits**: 当手柄抬头/低头在视角上、下限突然撞停时，为趋近边界的俯仰输入减速，同时让鼠标绕过此边界缓动；保留硬限位、反向离界响应和设备切换，不替代震动、速度 FOV 或基础 up 选择。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-gamepad-pitch-limits))
- **gameplay-generator**: Use when the user needs complex gameplay systems (skills, classes, quests, economy, buffs, mana, cooldowns). Triggers on: "add a skill system", "create player classes", "make a quest", "add mana/cooldown", "create a buff/debuff system", or dispatched by mc-mod-master. ([buyicoder/modfactory](https://github.com/buyicoder/modfactory/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-generator) · ★3)
- **gameplay-geometric-event-alignment**: 当切断等离散事件早于或晚于可见物体经过操作边界时，明确几何参照、触发条件与一次性状态，再核对实际事件位置；不负责动画加速或切割网格算法。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-geometric-event-alignment))
- **gameplay-gravity-orientation-cues**: 当墙面/天花板攀爬或旋转视角让世界向下方向难辨时，设计与真实重力一致的轻量运动线索及覆盖验收；不旋转相机、不改变角色重力，也不把普通落地尘或目标提示当同一任务。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-gravity-orientation-cues))
- **gameplay-grid-inventory-placement**: 为多格背包实现拖放吸附、整块足迹的边界/占用预览、可选直角旋转与有效落位提交；修复只检查锚点、旧格自碰撞和无效拖放丢物，不实现装备、合成、丢弃或堆叠规则。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-grid-inventory-placement))
- **gameplay-harness**: Use when a game needs an executable driver beside the runtime to inject semantic inputs, replay deterministic tapes, step the project's existing logic clock, and collect inspectable evidence. It does not decide what a session proves, replace game QA, or create a second gameplay clock. ([jammyfu/open-game-skills](https://github.com/jammyfu/open-game-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/disciplines/gameplay-harness) · ★14)
- **gameplay-health-damage-ui**: 在 HP 变化突兀、损失量难读或低血量提示不清时设计血条平滑、延迟损失段和生命分级；此旧入口保留兼容路由，瞬时浮字与结果总分计数各有独立 owner。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-health-damage-ui))
- **gameplay-highlights**: Use for OpenGGF work compilations, promotional reels, Stageplay or mod showcases, or refreshing highlights after fixes; not individual capture or parity testing. ([OpenGGF/OpenGGF](https://github.com/OpenGGF/OpenGGF/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/gameplay-highlights) · ★15 · 2 copies)
- **gameplay-hit-recovery**: 当连续受击使角色难以恢复操作时，评估一段时间内受击阈值脱身及其状态提示；单次受击后的免疫窗口另用独立入口，不将阈值脱身当作无敌。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-hit-recovery))
- **gameplay-hitstop**: 在命中缺少接触感、或发射/翻牌揭示缺少节奏时设计接触 hit-stop 与非接触表现停顿；不把冰冻状态或全局暂停混为一谈。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-hitstop))
- **gameplay-hold-to-multitap**: 为已支持按住完成的动作增加可选多次轻点入口，逐动作定义推进/取消语义并复用权威执行；保留原按住方式，不把持续防御、跳高或蓄力一律转换为连点。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-hold-to-multitap))
- **gameplay-hurtbox-tolerance**: 当攻击或危险障碍擦过角色边缘却命中、项目希望增加空间容差时，设计较小或贴合轮廓的受伤区域并验证命中边界；不把受伤区域等同于世界移动碰撞体，也不提供时间无敌。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-hurtbox-tolerance))
- **gameplay-impact-physics-activation**: 为大量未受扰动的采集道具建立静态待命到受击激活的物理交接，减少不必要的动态模拟；保留首击、碰撞与对象状态，不负责倒伏重心调参或自动休眠系统。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-impact-physics-activation))
- **gameplay-impact-visuals**: 改善接触闪光、冲击帧、粒子与碰撞文字，并管理既有战斗残留的寿命和遮挡预算；不把发射残留当命中，贴地余效生成与通用渲染实现不在本入口。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-impact-visuals))
- **gameplay-implementation**: Implement a gameplay mechanic from agreed rules into an existing engine project, including input, state transitions, lifecycle, feedback, persistence boundaries, and validation. Use for player actions, combat, interactions, inventory, progression, or NPC gameplay behavior. ([scarecr0w12/PlayWeld](https://github.com/scarecr0w12/PlayWeld/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/gameplay-implementation) · ★15)
- **gameplay-implementation-review**: Use when a PR or code change exists for a game project and you need to review it for both code quality AND design intent survival. Evolved from /game-code-review — adds Pass 0 checking whether design intent from handoff survived implementation. Not for reviewing design docs (use /game-review), not for feel evaluation (use /feel-pass), not for playability (use /build-playability-review). ([fagemx/gstack-game](https://github.com/fagemx/gstack-game/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-implementation-review) · ★83 · 5 copies)
- **gameplay-input-normalizer**: 当 $gameplay-design-orchestrator 已拥有一个游戏设计包的所有权、 且这是必需的首个下游阶段时使用；或者用于仅修订已锁定游戏玩法包的规范化层。 不要将其作为全新游戏构思的常规入口。 ([W-ChouYing/AISkills](https://github.com/W-ChouYing/AISkills/tree/HEAD/02-game-design-pipeline/gameplay-input-normalizer) · ★27)
- **gameplay-input-remapping-accessibility**: Audit input remapping and accessibility alternatives for conflict-free actions, device parity, and motor-access needs. ([Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code](https://github.com/Lucas-Belucci-Bellini/All-Skills-for-the-claude-code/tree/HEAD/curated-skill-sources/game-content-pipelines/gameplay-input-remapping-accessibility) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **gameplay-jump-forgiveness**: 当角色刚离开平台便无法起跳时，评估 coyote time 的窗口、消费与重置规则；不处理落地前命令缓冲、顶角纠偏或防止离台。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-jump-forgiveness))
- **gameplay-jump-horizontal-momentum**: 在一次合法起跳时按已声明方向策略替换或补充水平速度，修复不希望保留的漂移；区分地面/空中跳、来源速度和上限，不改变起跳资格、垂直高度或每帧空中加速。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-jump-horizontal-momentum))
- **gameplay-jump-input-buffer**: 当落地前略早按跳被丢弃时，设计有限期的跳跃命令保留、合法时机执行与一次性消费；不同于离地后延长起跳许可，不声称已有可直接移植的输入实现。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-jump-input-buffer))
- **gameplay-landing-feedback**: 当落地缺少重量或每帧接地重复播反馈时，绑定真实着地边沿并按高度/接触速度分级；不改跳高、接触物理或落地伤害。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-landing-feedback))
- **gameplay-landing-tumble-recovery**: 仅在项目明确要求坏落地的玩法后果时，以过快接触且不良接触角触发一次翻倒/布娃娃、短暂失控与持物掉落，再恢复控制和取回资格；不是普通落地特效，不自动添加伤害、敌人感知或噪声规则。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-landing-tumble-recovery))
- **gameplay-launch-pad-feedback**: 当环境跳板弹射缺少弹簧感、起飞与镜头脱节或接触重复触发时，编排跳板压缩、权威弹射、腾空与落地复位；不把视觉拉伸或翻转当跳高、额外跳跃或物理冲量。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-launch-pad-feedback))
- **gameplay-locomotion-contacts**: 当轮胎等移动部件漏掉路缘或侧面接触时，比较线采样与体积表示的覆盖、稳定性和成本；区分接触物理、网格外观与转向辅助。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-locomotion-contacts))
- **gameplay-loop-pacing**: Use when combat feels like repetitive kiting (running in circles), objectives stall pacing, or Last Stand timer gameplay feels passive. Focuses on anti-kiting pressure, engagement incentives, and objective pacing while preserving encounter identity. ([Excellund/AbyssalDescent](https://github.com/Excellund/AbyssalDescent/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/gameplay-loop-pacing))
- **gameplay-loot-pickup**: 当金币或掉落物的来源、引导和实际领取不清时，区分生成、可拾取、吸附与成功确认；吸附只在既有规则允许时使用，不猜半径、穿障或经济值。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-loot-pickup))
- **gameplay-low-health-damage-reduction**: 在残血时下一次普通命中立即结束尝试、希望增加有限生存机会时，设计按生命状态缩放伤害的规则与边界测试；不负责固定 1 HP 保命、血条显示或受击无敌窗口。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-low-health-damage-reduction))
- **gameplay-mechanic**: Читай, когда реализуешь игровую механику по её доке — движение, стрельбу, врагов, физику, взаимодействие, всё, что игрок делает или чувствует. Здесь про порядок работы через MCP, устройство кода механики, дешёвый цикл проверки после правки скрипта и про то, что показывать пользователю на приёмку. ([IgorGribowsky/rts-sandbox](https://github.com/IgorGribowsky/rts-sandbox/tree/HEAD/rts-sandbox-src/.claude/skills/gameplay-mechanic))
- **gameplay-mechanical-interactions**: 当压板、开关与门体运动/通行状态不一致时，组织触发、机械位移和接地反馈；分段运动、占用和中途反向按明确状态规则处理。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-mechanical-interactions))
- **gameplay-mechanics**: Core gameplay mechanics implementation, system interactions, feedback loops, and iterative balance refinement for engaging player experiences. ([pluginagentmarketplace/custom-plugin-game-developer](https://github.com/pluginagentmarketplace/custom-plugin-game-developer/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-mechanics) · ★56 · 2 copies)
- **gameplay-mechanics**: Use when implementing or refining concrete gameplay mechanics, feedback loops, combat, economy, progression, movement, event interactions, or balance iteration. ([nil-studio/oasis7](https://github.com/nil-studio/oasis7/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-mechanics) · ★3)
- **gameplay-mechanism-designer**: Expand a game-design seed, object, mechanic, image, scene, reference, or rough pitch into structurally distinct gameplay, mechanism chains, repeatable loops, connected systems, and testable prototypes. Use for gameplay ideation, mechanic mutation, reverse-design from visuals, core-loop or system design, prototype planning, and gameplay audits; do not use for purely narrative or visual development that does not require playable rules. ([qiuaoru-coder/game-design-agent-skills](https://github.com/qiuaoru-coder/game-design-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/gameplay-mechanism-designer) · ★8)
- **gameplay-melee-feedback**: 为普通近战命中、受击、暴击及重武器重量组合共享反馈；剑轨迹、弹反、踩踏、攻击方向规则和受击脱身使用独立入口，不凭视觉改变判定。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-melee-feedback))
- **gameplay-motion-deformation**: 在接触、弹跳、按钮反馈或快速出招缺少受力和轮廓时设计 squash/stretch、回弹、视觉后坐与 smear；只管理事件形变，持续跟随、瞄准组合、动作时序和物理接触使用独立入口。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-motion-deformation))
- **gameplay-movement-feedback**: 当四向/八向移动动画或跑姿与真实运动朝向不一致时校对方向表现；冲刺、落地、跳跃许可和车辆物理使用独立入口，不宣称方向帧增加就降低输入延迟。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-movement-feedback))
- **gameplay-movement-look-decoupling**: 当观察镜头强制决定角色移动/朝向、妨碍边走边看时，分离移动意图与观察输入，定义移动中及静止时的朝向契约；不是方向帧增加、攻击瞄准、自动转向或相机 up 稳定。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-movement-look-decoupling))
- **gameplay-multiray-object-selection**: 让解谜或探索游戏的小型交互物更容易选中：在准星附近圆形区域采样多条射线，尊重遮挡并按离准星中心的距离选唯一目标；不转动相机、不提供射击磁吸或自动执行交互。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-multiray-object-selection))
- **gameplay-navmesh**: NavMesh and pathfinding specification for BlastSimulator2026: 2D NavGrid derived from VoxelGrid surface, A* with 8-directional movement and octile heuristic, multi-level ramp routing, and dynamic incremental updates after blasts. Use alongside img2threejs for reference reading and threejs-scene-authoring for explicit hand placement and headed verification. ([vibegameengine/web-starter-kit](https://github.com/vibegameengine/web-starter-kit/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/gameplay-space-composition) · ★151)
- **gameplay-spec**: Produce an implementation-ready gameplay specification covering player goal, rules, states, inputs, feedback, tunable data, interactions, failure, edge cases, and validation without dictating unnecessary code structure. ([Dadmin88/hermes-profile-packs](https://github.com/Dadmin88/hermes-profile-packs/tree/HEAD/hermes-agency/profiles/agency-game-designer/skills/gameplay-spec) · ★49)
- **gameplay-speed-camera**: 在钩爪、冲刺或跑酷已有高速运动，但速度线、动态 FOV 与径向模糊缺少协调或妨碍路径判断时设计持续速度表现；不把镜头变化当成角色实际加速。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-speed-camera))
- **gameplay-sprite-normal-orientation**: 精灵翻面、旋转或换方向帧后法线光照反向、跳变或与表面不符时，检查贴图配对、法线空间与渲染器已有变换，只在缺失处修正一次；不把每个镜像方向都变成新法线资产，也不接管角色朝向或灯的控制。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-sprite-normal-orientation))
- **gameplay-state-driven-surface-motion**: 当机器已运行但皮带等表面仍静止，或材质滚动与实际运行状态矛盾时，限定目标表面并将权威状态映射到可读的表面动画；不是实体皮带模拟、机关通行或任意背景 UV 装饰。 ([ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills](https://github.com/ziyou-lu/bkb-gameplay-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/gameplay-state-driven-surface-motion))
- **gameplay-state-machine-implementation**: Designs and implements deterministic hierarchical state machines for player states, game phases, and quest logic.
